All bind ACQUIRED-20261017-A1. Current cumulative versions are projected after the historical V01 projection below. New retrospective interpretations are not represented as claims frozen before their supporting source was read.

All V01 rows bind `ACQUIRED-20261017-A1`, its source/hash/locator identity, and `O### = LN-JP-V01-O###` in the [complete frozen reading](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_LN_V01_DEEP_READING.md). The reading owns full observation text, source-order freezes and original prediction wording. F-states name disclosure order, not fictional chronology. The initial claims below reproduce their original bounded formulations; endpoint revisions explicitly show what changes. The reading's retrospective argument is the current argument home within this boundary until a separately accepted specialist exists. It is not evidence for another edition.

## Responsibility and stable identities

This is the single canonical home for adjudication and revision history. Source observations remain in their unit readings, cumulative state in the three other ledgers, and developed arguments in their accepted specialist/integrated homes. Use `CLM-<unit>-NNN.vN`, `PRED-<unit>-NNN` and `V1X-NNN`, where the inventory's unit ID already identifies its witness lane and the record additionally binds its edition revision. Preserve prior formulations, dated source boundary, evidence set, and decision rationale. A current projection points to the latest version without deleting history.

Distinguish `SOURCE_FACT`, `INTERPRETATION`, `HYPOTHESIS`, `UNCERTAINTY`, and `NORMATIVE_EVALUATION`. A narrator's explicit statement is a source fact about a statement; its objective truth may be a hypothesis. Confidence is a reasoned qualitative assessment and does not replace evidence.

Standard revision vocabulary:

| State | Decision meaning |
| --- | --- |
| PRESERVE | The bounded earlier claim survives the new evidence without material change |
| STRENGTHEN | Additional independent support or resolved alternatives justify greater confidence or a supported extension |
| REVISE | Meaning, mechanism, scope or temporal applicability materially changes |
| DOWNGRADE | Support weakens or alternatives strengthen; retain only the warranted confidence or scope |
| REJECT | The bounded claim is contradicted or untenable; preserve its record and rejection evidence |
| OPEN | Evidence is insufficient or competing explanations remain unresolved |

These are evidence dispositions, not a forced linear progression. Novelty earns no credit. An uncertainty can remain OPEN at final release.

## V1-to-V2 crosswalk schema — intentionally empty

| Crosswalk ID | V1 exact path / section / claim quotation or bounded paraphrase | Independent V2 freeze / evidence and locators | Supported current interpretation / authoritative location | Strongest counterevidence / alternatives | PRESERVE / STRENGTHEN / REVISE / DOWNGRADE / REJECT / OPEN | Rationale / unresolved disagreement / next evidence |
| --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- | --- |

Procedure: finish and durably freeze independent V2 claims for the relevant source boundary; then open only the needed V1 artifact, identify its claim precisely, and compare source-grounded support. Record material differences in witnesses/coverage before treating a disagreement as interpretive. The historical transcript's uncertain first-turn capture is a provenance limitation, not a gap to fill from imagination. V1 remains historically accessible even when V2 rejects a claim.

## Transaction acceptance

Adjudicate claims affected by the new unit; link changes to current states and authoritative prose; preserve unresolved alternatives and prediction failures. No unexamined V1 claim may be represented as a V2 expectation. Comparison sessions cannot contaminate the still-unread independent lane: use separate contexts and record exposure. Revision 0.1: schema only.

## ERRATUM-KSS-05-001 — housing and room-privacy routing

The LN09 update CLM-LN-JP-V04-005.v5 changed the subject of an established housing-agreement claim to room privacy. This is an identifier-routing error, not evidence that the housing claim failed. Fifty-four LN09 older-claim update pairs were screened for identity: this one break was confirmed; the other53 identity routes were preserved, without claiming full semantic reacceptance. CLM-LN-JP-V03-001 received a separate v1–v7 history review and remains a legitimate gradual contextual development.

The v5 row and all accepted readings, freezes, audits and original model prefixes remain byte-exact. Current v6 formulations above restore the housing home and place the valid privacy evidence in its existing privacy home. The revision is retrospective and attributable; it adds no prospective knowledge or heldout credit.

## ERRATUM-OFF01-SOURCE-001 — physical container fate

OFF01 correspondence exposed an unsupported physical-disposal inference in the preserved V05 reading and its derived records. Original `LN-JP-V05 / item/xhtml/p-010.xhtml / paragraph61`, serialization SHA-256 `c125c61cb0121097c9a200a29d2ad9d017c339948d9e292e393573830c2f58d8`, states that Sendai opened the no-opening can with a can opener for the room contract, it ceased to function as a piggybank, and she did not bring it to the new room. It does not establish discard or final physical location. The seven bounded literal hits for 貯金箱/捨て/処分 and their complete checked contexts supply no explicit can-disposal statement; this is not universal absence across every wording or witness.

Current controlling qualifications are `CHR-LN-JP-V05-SENDAI_HAZUKI-001.v2`, `REL-LN-JP-V05-021.v2` and `PROP-LN-JP-V02-009.v7`. Their older v1/v1/v4 disposal formulations remain recoverable historical rows, not unqualified current physical-fate authority. All other original welcome, housing, furnishing, funding and later spending facts remain. The physical can is distinct from its former contents and the later reimbursed fund: fund persistence does not prove container relocation. No final disposal, storage elsewhere or continued usability is invented.

The frozen [V05 reading](../02%20Sequential%20Readings/SHUUKURA_V2_LN_V05_DEEP_READING.md) remains byte-identical; this erratum qualifies its disposal wording without rewriting prospective history. Original `LN05p004247–254` distinguishes partial contract expenditure and half-contract reimbursement; `LN07p005317` records later ticket spending. These later facts qualify a timeless reading of OFF01 R167 while leaving OFF01 historical no-opening resolve and undecided note fate intact. Evidence: OFF-JP-SS01-O004–005 and the exact original-context receipt retained outside analytical Git. Disposition: REVISE unsupported disposal to opened/lost function/not-brought/final disposition unestablished; retain uncertainty and every frozen source boundary.
